Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">SANTA CLARA, Calif., May&#160;14, 2026 &#8212; Applied Materials, Inc. (NASDAQ&#58; AMAT) today reported results for its second quarter ended Apr. 26, 2026.</font></div><div style="margin-top:15pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Second Quarter Results</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Applied generated record revenue of $7.91 billion. On a GAAP basis, the company reported gross margin of 49.9 percent,  operating income of $2.52 billion or 31.9 percent of revenue, and record earnings per share (EPS) of $3.51.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">On a non-GAAP basis, the company reported gross margin of 50.0 percent, operating income of $2.54 billion or 32.1 percent of revenue, and record EPS of $2.86.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The company generated $845 million in cash from operations and distributed $765 million to shareholders through $400 million in share repurchases and $365 million in dividends.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8220;Applied Materials delivered record quarterly performance, and we now expect our semiconductor equipment business to grow more than 30 percent in calendar 2026,&#8221; said Gary Dickerson, President and CEO. &#8220;The rapid global build-out of AI computing infrastructure combined with Applied&#8217;s strong leadership positions in leading-edge logic, DRAM and advanced packaging provide an exceptionally strong foundation for sustained, multi-year revenue and profit growth.&#8221;</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8220;The growth in AI that Applied has been investing for is now in full force,&#8221; said Brice Hill, Senior Vice President and CFO. &#8220;As the largest process equipment company in the fastest growing markets, our top priority is ensuring we have the operational and supply chain readiness to support our customers&#8217; growth. We have increased our build plan, inventory positions and logistics capacity, and we are driving higher operating profit and productivity across the company.&#8221;</font></div><div These engagements build upon the previously announced partnership with Samsung Electronics.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">A new innovation partnership with TSMC to accelerate the development and commercialization of semiconductor technologies required for the next era of AI. Working together at Applied&#8217;s EPIC Center in Silicon Valley, the companies will co-innovate to advance materials engineering, equipment innovation, and process integration technologies designed to deliver energy-efficient performance from the data center to the edge.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Arizona State University (ASU), Rensselaer Polytechnic Institute (RPI) and Stanford University will join Applied&#8217;s EPIC Center as inaugural research partners, leveraging the synergy of academia and industry to accelerate energy&#8209;efficient innovations for next&#8209;generation AI chips.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Advantest Corporation, a leading semiconductor test equipment supplier, will join Applied&#8217;s EPIC platform as an innovation partner to strengthen the links between front-end manufacturing technologies and back-end testing of chips and packages, helping chipmakers bring new designs to market faster.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">A long-term collaboration agreement between Applied and SK hynix to accelerate the development and deployment of next-generation DRAM and high-bandwidth memory (HBM) essential for AI and high-performance computing. Engineers from both companies will work side-by-side at Applied&#8217;s EPIC Center to advance innovation in materials, process integration and 3D advanced packaging as memory architectures move beyond current production nodes.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Applied and Micron Technology are working to develop next-generation DRAM, HBM and NAND solutions that increase the energy-efficient performance of AI systems, bringing together advanced R&#38;D capabilities from Applied&#8217;s EPIC Center in Silicon Valley and Micron&#8217;s state-of-the-art innovation center in Boise, Idaho.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Introduced chip-making systems designed to create the smallest atomic-scale features in 3D Gate-All-Around transistors for the world&#8217;s most advanced logic chips. By controlling materials deposition with atomic-level </font></div><div style="height:45pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:center"><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:45pt;width:100%"><div style="margin-top:3pt;text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Applied Materials, Inc.</font></div><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Page 3 of 14</font></div></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">precision, the technologies enable chipmakers to build faster and more power-efficient transistors at the scale required to sustain the pace of today&#8217;s global AI infrastructure build-out.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Precision&#8482; Selective Nitride PECVD preserves integrity of shallow trench isolation, reducing parasitic capacitance and boosting chip performance-per-watt.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:63pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Trillium&#8482; ALD wraps silicon nanosheets with complex metal gate stacks that optimize transistors for a wide range of AI computing applications.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Entered into an agreement with ASMPT Limited to acquire its NEXX business, a leading supplier of large-area advanced packaging deposition equipment for the semiconductor industry. The addition of the NEXX team and products will broaden Applied&#8217;s portfolio of panel-level advanced packaging technologies which are designed to enable chipmakers and systems companies to build larger-body AI accelerators for higher energy-efficient performance.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Received a 2026 Intel EPIC Supplier Award for Excellence in Technology Development.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Joined Synopsys and NVIDIA in a collaboration to advance AI and quantum chemistry R&#38;D with accelerated materials modeling.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt;padding-left:27pt;text-indent:-6.75pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:2.9pt">Increased the quarterly cash dividend by 15 percent, from $0.46 to $0.53 per share, marking nine consecutive years of dividend increases. With the increase, Applied has more than doubled its dividend per share from four years ago.</font></div><div style="height:45pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div Measures</font></div><div><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:112%">Applied provides investors with certain non-GAAP financial measures, which are adjusted for the impact of certain costs, expenses, gains and losses, including, as applicable, certain items related to mergers and acquisitions&#59; restructuring and severance charges and any associated adjustments&#59; legal settlement charges&#59; impairments of assets&#59; gain or loss, dividends and impairments on strategic investments&#59; certain income tax items&#59; and other discrete adjustments. On a non-GAAP basis, the tax effect related to share-based compensation is recognized ratably over the fiscal year. Reconciliations of these non-GAAP measures to the most directly comparable financial measures calculated and presented in accordance with GAAP are provided in the financial tables included in this release.</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Management uses these non-GAAP financial measures to evaluate the company&#8217;s operating and financial performance and for planning purposes, and as performance measures in its executive compensation program. Applied believes these measures enhance an overall understanding of its performance and investors&#8217; ability to review the company&#8217;s business from the same perspective as the company&#8217;s management, and facilitate comparisons of this period&#8217;s results with prior periods on a consistent basis by excluding items that management does not believe are indicative of Applied's ongoing operating performance. There are limitations in using non-GAAP financial measures because the non-GAAP financial measures are not prepared in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles, may be different from non-GAAP financial measures used by other companies, and may exclude certain items that may have a material impact upon our reported financial results. The presentation of this additional information is not meant to be considered in isolation or as a substitute for the directly comparable financial measures prepared in accordance with GAAP.</font></div><div style="margin-top:14pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Webcast Information</font></div><div style="margin-top:6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Applied Materials will discuss these results during an earnings call that begins at 1&#58;30 p.m. Pacific Time today.
